3661286       04:22 lelinfe+ /bin/bash -lc source /usr/local/Ascend/ascend-toolkit/set_env.sh export LD_LIBRARY_PATH=/usr/lib/aarch64-linux-gnu:${LD_LIBRARY_PATH:-} export LC_ALL=C base='????????????/R-W4-4/V090/partial-ranking' local_log="$base/v090-local-command-results.log" sb_log="$base/v090-samebinary-command-results.log" printf 'REVISION=V090\nGATE=INTERLEAVED_LOCAL\nPARENT=R31B-V011\nDEVICE=4\nDTYPE=FP32\nSHAPES=128x1024,128x1032,128x1040\nWARMUP=45\nSAMPLES_PER_BLOCK=31\nBLOCKS=2\nGAP_SEC=0\nBATCH_N=64\nPAIRS_PER_SHAPE=6\nORDER_PER_SHAPE=01:P,C;02:C,P;03:P,C;04:C,P;05:P,C;06:C,P\nPARTIAL_CORRECTNESS=YES\nLOCAL_SCORE_COMPARABLE_TO_OFFICIAL=NO\nC15=EXCLUDED_KNOWN_PARENT_FAILURE\n' > "$local_log" printf 'REVISION=V090\nGATE=PARENT_SAME_BINARY_CONTEXT\nDEVICE=4\nSHAPES=128x1024,128x1032,128x1040\nPRECHECK_A_REPS=3\nPRECHECK_B_REPS=3\nWARMUP=45\nSAMPLES_PER_BLOCK=31\nBLOCKS=2\nBATCH_N=64\n' > "$sb_log" failed=0 run_one() {   variant=$1   width=$2   prefix=$3   output_log=$4   if [ "$variant" = parent ]; then probe="$base/stage/build-v090/clx_ref_parent_probe"; else probe="$base/stage/build-v090/clx_ref_candidate_probe"; fi   start_utc=$(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S.%NZ)   printf 'START_UTC=%s\nCOMMAND=%s 4 128 %s 0 %s 45 31 2 0 64\n' "$start_utc" "$probe" "$width" "$prefix" >> "$output_log"   "$probe" 4 128 "$width" 0 "$prefix" 45 31 2 0 64 > "${prefix}-stdout.log" 2> "${prefix}-stderr.log"   rc=$?   end_utc=$(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S.%NZ)   bad=$(awk -F '\t' '$1=="bad" {print $2; exit}' "${prefix}-stats.txt" 2>/dev/null)   max_abs=$(awk -F '\t' '$1=="max_abs" {print $2; exit}' "${prefix}-stats.txt" 2>/dev/null)   [ -n "$bad" ] || bad=UNKNOWN   [ -n "$max_abs" ] || max_abs=UNKNOWN   printf 'RESULT stage=%s variant=%s shape=128x%s rc=%s bad=%s max_abs=%s\nEND_UTC=%s\n' "$output_log" "$variant" "$width" "$rc" "$bad" "$max_abs" "$end_utc" >> "$output_log"   printf 'rc=%s bad=%s max_abs=%s\n' "$rc" "$bad" "$max_abs" > "${prefix}-result.txt"   if [ "$rc" -ne 0 ] || [ "$bad" != 0 ]; then failed=1; fi } for width in 1024 1032 1040; do   npu-smi info -t usages -i 4 -c 0 > "$base/v090-sb-w${width}-A-pre-usage.txt" 2>&1   for rep in 01 02 03; do     prefix="$base/v090-samebinary-A-rep${rep}-parent-r128-w${width}-fp32"     run_one parent "$width" "$prefix" "$sb_log"   done   sleep 2   npu-smi info -t usages -i 4 -c 0 > "$base/v090-sb-w${width}-B-pre-usage.txt" 2>&1   for rep in 01 02 03; do     prefix="$base/v090-samebinary-B-rep${rep}-parent-r128-w${width}-fp32"     run_one parent "$width" "$prefix" "$sb_log"   done done for width in 1024 1032 1040; do   for pair in 1 2 3 4 5 6; do     printf -v pair_id '%02d' "$pair"     snapshot_prefix="$base/v090-local-pair${pair_id}-r128-w${width}-fp32"     npu-smi info -t usages -i 4 -c 0 > "${snapshot_prefix}-pre-usage.txt" 2>&1     npu-smi info > "${snapshot_prefix}-pre-npu-smi-info.txt" 2>&1     ps -eo pid,etime,user,args > "${snapshot_prefix}-pre-processes.txt"     if [ $((pair % 2)) -eq 1 ]; then       run_one parent "$width" "$base/v090-local-pair${pair_id}-parent-r128-w${width}-fp32" "$local_log"       run_one candidate "$width" "$base/v090-local-pair${pair_id}-candidate-r128-w${width}-fp32" "$local_log"     else       run_one candidate "$width" "$base/v090-local-pair${pair_id}-candidate-r128-w${width}-fp32" "$local_log"       run_one parent "$width" "$base/v090-local-pair${pair_id}-parent-r128-w${width}-fp32" "$local_log"     fi     npu-smi info -t usages -i 4 -c 0 > "${snapshot_prefix}-post-usage.txt" 2>&1     npu-smi info > "${snapshot_prefix}-post-npu-smi-info.txt" 2>&1     ps -eo pid,etime,user,args > "${snapshot_prefix}-post-processes.txt"   done done exit "$failed"
